- **openspec-aware**: OpenSpec-mode authoring for Chorus PM workflows in Claude Code. The default whenever OpenSpec is usable; consumes the resolved `## Spec Mode` (never re-detects). Scaffolds `openspec/changes/&lt;slug&gt;/` on disk, and mirrors Markdown files into Chorus document drafts via `chorus mcp call --arg-file` (bash `chorus-api.sh` wrapper as fallback). Required reading for the proposal, develop, and yolo skills. When OpenSpec is not the resolved mode, this skill no-ops and the caller follows the resolved mode (spec-lite or free-form). ([Chorus-AIDLC/Chorus](https://github.com/Chorus-AIDLC/Chorus/tree/HEAD/public/chorus-plugin/skills/openspec-aware) · ★1.2k)
- **openspec-aware**: OpenSpec-mode authoring for Chorus PM workflows on OpenClaw — the default whenever OpenSpec is usable. Resolves the whole spec-mode contract (lite/openspec/off) by sourcing the plugin's shipped `bin/resolve-spec-mode.sh` (no SessionStart hook on OpenClaw), scaffolds `openspec/changes/&lt;slug&gt;/` on disk, and mirrors Markdown files into Chorus document drafts via `chorus mcp call --arg-file` (bash `chorus-api.sh` wrapper as fallback). When OpenSpec isn't usable the mode resolves to spec-lite (see the spec-lite skill). Required reading for the proposal, develop, and yolo skills. ([Chorus-AIDLC/Chorus](https://github.com/Chorus-AIDLC/Chorus/tree/HEAD/packages/openclaw-plugin/skills/openspec-aware) · ★1.2k)

Required reading for the proposal, develop, and yolo skills. When OpenSpec is not the resolved mode, this skill no-ops and the caller follows the resolved mode (spec-lite or free-form). - **openspec-aware**: Use this skill BEFORE any planning, brainstorming, write-plan, or execute-plan activity in a project that has an openspec/ directory. Checks for existing OpenSpec change proposals that match the user's request and treats them as authoritative input, preventing duplicate planning work and spec drift. Activates automatically when the user asks to plan, build, implement, or start work on a feature in a spec-driven project. - **openspec-aware-chorus**: OpenSpec-mode authoring for Chorus PM workflows on dsh — the default whenever OpenSpec is usable. Consumes the `## Spec Mode` block the chorus-dsh bundle injects (mode resolved once at load by `src/spec-mode.ts`), scaffolds `openspec/changes/&lt;slug&gt;/` on disk, and mirrors Markdown files into Chorus document drafts via `chorus mcp call --arg-file` (package-local `chorus-mcp-call.mjs` wrapper as fallback).
